\section*{Proof of \texttt{totally-bounded-has-cauchy-subsequence}}
\textbf{Claim.}
\begin{align*}
  \forall s.\; (\mathsf{totally{-}bounded}(s) \Rightarrow \forall f \in (\mathbb{N} \to x(s)).\; \exists \varphi.\; (\mathsf{strictly{-}mono{-}nn}(\varphi) \wedge \mathsf{is{-}cauchy{-}seq}(s, \mathit{subseq}(f, \varphi))))
\end{align*}
\bigskip

\subsection*{Tactics used}
\begin{itemize}
\item \texttt{sp} -- start the proof: install the claim as the initial goal
\item \texttt{di} -- direct inference: break the goal at its top connective -- move an implication's hypothesis into the assumptions, split a conjunction, or introduce a universally quantified variable
\item \texttt{ai} -- antecedent inference: decompose a structured assumption, named by its number or its formula
\item \texttt{mac-h} -- macete in a hypothesis: unfold a defined predicate inside an assumption
\item \texttt{ass} -- assert: close the goal directly from the assumptions
\item \texttt{mac} -- macete: rewrite the goal using a named theorem or definition
\item \texttt{inst+} -- instantiate and detach: specialise a universal assumption at a term, then discharge its in-context guards
\item \texttt{cut} -- cut: introduce an intermediate lemma, discharged as a side goal
\end{itemize}

\subsection*{Notation}
\begin{itemize}
\item $\Rightarrow$ -- implies
\item $\in$ -- set membership
\item $(A \to B)$ -- the set of functions from $A$ to $B$
\item $\mathbb{N}$ -- the natural numbers
\item $\wedge$ -- and
\item $\mathbb{R}$ -- the real numbers
\item $\neg$ -- not
\item $|x|$ -- the cardinality (number of elements) of the set $x$
\item $\subseteq$ -- subset
\item $\mathrm{Inf}(x)$ -- the infinite subsets of $x$
\item \texttt{totally-bounded}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{strictly-mono-nn}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{is-cauchy-seq}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{pos-rr}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{is-r-net}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{is-finite-cover}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\item \texttt{null-rr-seq}$(\ldots)$ -- a defined term; see the Definitions reference (DEFINITIONS.md) for its full definition
\end{itemize}

Each step shows the tactic applied; the goal nodes it opens and which one is now in focus (the bracketed \texttt{[k]} of the workspace display); the focus node's assumptions (\textbf{A1}, \textbf{A2}, \dots{}, the numbering the tactic args refer to); and the goal $G$ it leaves, written $\vdash G$.  Each sequent is an \texttt{align*} environment: break a long formula by hand with \texttt{\textbackslash\textbackslash\ \&} and indent at the alignment column.
